```ts
  317 |       // Vietnamese Language consistency
  318 |       await loginAsAdmin(page);
  319 |       await page.getByText('Người dùng').click();
  320 |       
  321 |       // Page elements check
  322 |       await expect(page.locator('h2')).toHaveText('Quản lý Người dùng');
  323 |       await expect(page.getByText('Đăng xuất')).toBeVisible();
  324 |       await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xóa' }).first()).toBeVisible();
  325 |       return;
  326 |     }
  327 | 
  328 |     if (tc.caseId === 'F19-TC-010') {
  329 |       // Delete standard user
  330 |       await loginAsAdmin(page);
  331 |       await page.getByText('Người dùng').click();
  332 | 
  333 |       const userRow = page.locator('table tbody tr', { hasText: tc.email || '' });
  334 |       await expect(userRow).toBeVisible();
  335 |       
  336 |       // Click delete on the target user's row
  337 |       await userRow.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xóa' }).click();
  338 |       
  339 |       // Wait for table to reload and confirm user is removed
  340 |       await expect(userRow).toBeHidden({ timeout: 5000 });
  341 |       return;
  342 |     }
  343 | 
  344 |     if (tc.caseId === 'F19-TC-011') {
  345 |       // Prevent Self-Deletion (UI)
  346 |       await loginAsAdmin(page);
  347 |       await page.getByText('Người dùng').click();
  348 | 
  349 |       const adminRow = page.locator('table tbody tr', { hasText: 'admin@eshop.com' });
  350 |       await expect(adminRow).toBeVisible();
  351 |       
  352 |       // Verify delete button is disabled or hidden for the currently logged in admin user
  353 |       const deleteBtn = adminRow.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xóa' });
  354 |       // SUT failure expectation (Bug): SUT renders the Xóa button active for admin.
  355 |       // The assertion below checks for disabled or hidden status as per the FR-19 spec.
  356 |       const isDisabled = await deleteBtn.isDisabled();
  357 |       const isHidden = await deleteBtn.isHidden();
  358 |       expect(isDisabled || isHidden).toBe(true);
  359 |       return;
  360 |     }
  361 | 
  362 |     if (tc.caseId === 'F19-TC-012') {
  363 |       // Prevent Self-Deletion (API)
  364 |       const adminLogin = await requestContext.post(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/login`, {
  365 |         data: ADMIN_CREDENTIALS
  366 |       });
  367 |       const adminData = await adminLogin.json();
  368 |       const adminToken = adminData.token;
  369 | 
  370 |       // Request self-deletion via API
  371 |       const res = await requestContext.delete(`${API_BASE_URL}/api/admin/users/${adminUserId}`, {
  372 |         headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${adminToken}` }
  373 |       });
  374 |       
  375 |       // SUT failure expectation (Bug): SUT returns 200 and deletes the admin!
  376 |       // Re-seed admin user immediately if deletion succeeded to keep DB state clean
  377 |       if (res.status() === 200) {
  378 |         console.warn('SUT deleted the logged-in admin! Recreating admin user to prevent database corruption...');
  379 |         reseedAdminSync();
  380 |       }
  381 | 
  382 |       // Per specification, self-deletion must be blocked (400 or 403)
  383 |       expect(res.status()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(400);
  384 |       return;
  385 |     }
  386 | 
  387 |     if (tc.caseId === 'F19-TC-013') {
  388 |       // Delete button color is red
  389 |       await loginAsAdmin(page);
  390 |       await page.getByText('Người dùng').click();
  391 | 
  392 |       const deleteBtn = page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xóa' }).first();
  393 |       await expect(deleteBtn).toBeVisible();
  394 | 
  395 |       // Verify class contains bg-red-500 or style has red background-color
  396 |       const className = await deleteBtn.getAttribute('class') || '';
  397 |       const isTailwindRed = className.includes('bg-red-');
  398 |       const styleBg = await deleteBtn.evaluate(el => window.getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor);
  399 |       const isRGBRed = styleBg.includes('rgb(239, 68, 68)') || styleBg.includes('rgb(220, 38, 38)') || styleBg.includes('rgb(185, 28, 28)');
  400 |       
  401 |       expect(isTailwindRed || isRGBRed).toBe(true);
  402 |       return;
  403 |     }
  404 | 
  405 |     if (tc.caseId === 'F19-TC-014') {
  406 |       // Page heading: exactly one h1 per page describing content
  407 |       await loginAsAdmin(page);
  408 |       await page.getByText('Người dùng').click();
```
